# GRID_TOP_CANDIDATES - trigger x bar candidates so far (W-GRIDA), refreshed 2026-11-24 03:04Z

## NOTICE 1 (W-LEVEL, 13:20Z) - POINT-AND-FIGURE (pnf_*) LOOK-AHEAD CHECK: NO LOOK-AHEAD IN THE BAR CONSTRUCTION
- Test: an independent causal replay of the P&F rule (box = p x trailing-20-day median range scale, excluding today; 3-box reversal) on 4 trading days of NQ prints (202606), session by session, versus the stored pnf_14 bars: column count, column extreme (close) and the bar's known_ts (= the print that confirms the reversal) agree in every complete session (20 of 20; the 21st is a session cut by my day-file window, not a bar error). The fill (fpx/fts) is the first print strictly after known_ts in every checked bar.
- What the bar IS: a column is emitted only when price has moved 3 boxes against it; its close is the column EXTREME and its time is the confirming print. So at the signal the market is already 3 boxes away from the "close" and the entry is taken there (first print after known_ts + slippage). That is causal but it makes every pnf signal a "after a 3-box reversal" entry, so break-type triggers on pnf bars enter after the reversal, not at the break. No future information is used.
- Still to test (W-LEVEL): robustness of the pnf level cells to a 1 s / 5 s fill delay, and whether the pnf winners are just "enter 3 boxes after the extreme" (a momentum-after-reversal effect that other bar types do not have). Until then pnf level cells carry the caution flag "construction-specific".

VERDICT (data ends 2026-11-12; SCREEN-LEVEL: naked trigger, no layers, no engine, points per 1 lot at $4.84 incl, not dollars of the live book): 713 candidates = every top-20 cell per session of every scored pass-1 refresh (union kept); ranked by prior years 2023-25 positive, then persistence, then last-6-month points; null excess shows NO NULL YET until null files pass the audit (>=5 accepted draws); nothing here is a LEAD.

**COUNT LINE: of 713 candidates, 548 have >= 5 audited random-entry draws and 355 of those beat ALL their draws (L6M). beat all: 355 (scalp-size <= 60/60: 44, wide > 60: 168, bracket not audited yet: 143); with >= 5 draws: 548 (scalp-size <= 60/60: 69, wide > 60: 252, bracket not audited yet: 227). CAUTION: this null is CELL-LEVEL (the same cell with random entries of the same count): a candidate was picked as the best of millions of cells, so beating one random draw of its own cell does NOT correct for that selection; the selection-corrected test is the best-pick null of the family tables on DS3_SIGNAL_BAR_GRID.md. rc (random-clock) and shifted-label nulls are not done for the candidates yet.**

## Plain-word key for the bar codes
| code | meaning | build rule (BAR_REGISTRY.md) |
|---|---|---|
| tm60/tm180/tm300/tm600 ... | live-style clock bars (seconds in the code: tm180 = 3-minute) | epoch-aligned time bar, closes on the clock |
| time_s | time | seconds bar: bucket = floor((ts - first trade of session)/P); empty buckets give no bar; known at bucket end |
| time_m | time | minute bar, same anchoring (note NT8 anchors minute bars to the session template start; ours reset at each of our session breaks). 1 = REFERENCE ONLY |
| vol | activity | volume bar: closes when cumulative contracts >= N; closing print belongs to the closing bar (NT8 splits the closing print across bars; see C) |
| tick | activity | tick-count bar: closes after N trade prints (MBO T records, one per matched trade); closing print belongs to the closing bar |
| dol | activity | dollar bar: closes when cumulative price*20*size >= N x 20 x 15,000 (N in contracts-equivalent at 15,000) |
| vsess | activity | volume-clock / session-relative: N equal-volume bars per session; bar volume = trailing median session volume / N (a session with a typical volume curve gets N bars; NY open fast, lunch slow in clock time) |
| tib | information | tick-imbalance bar (Lopez de Prado): theta = sum b_t*w_t (b = aggressor side, tick rule if none; w = 1); close when /theta/ >= E[T]*max(/EWMA(b w)/, EWMA/w//sqrt(E[T])); E[T] = EWMA(0.2) of bar lengths clamped [T0/4,4 T0]; state r |
| vib | information | volume-imbalance bar (Lopez de Prado): theta = sum b_t*w_t (b = aggressor side, tick rule if none; w = size); close when /theta/ >= E[T]*max(/EWMA(b w)/, EWMA/w//sqrt(E[T])); E[T] = EWMA(0.2) of bar lengths clamped [T0/4,4 T0]; st |
| dib | information | dollar-imbalance bar (Lopez de Prado): theta = sum b_t*w_t (b = aggressor side, tick rule if none; w = dollars); close when /theta/ >= E[T]*max(/EWMA(b w)/, EWMA/w//sqrt(E[T])); E[T] = EWMA(0.2) of bar lengths clamped [T0/4,4 T0]; |
| trb | information | tick-run bar (Lopez de Prado): close when max(sum of w over buys, sum over sells) >= E[T]*max(P(buy)E[w/buy],P(sell)E[w/sell]) |
| vrb | information | volume-run bar (Lopez de Prado): close when max(sum of w over buys, sum over sells) >= E[T]*max(P(buy)E[w/buy],P(sell)E[w/sell]) |
| drb | information | dollar-run bar (Lopez de Prado): close when max(sum of w over buys, sum over sells) >= E[T]*max(P(buy)E[w/buy],P(sell)E[w/sell]) |
| dlt | information | delta bar: closes when /cumulative aggressor delta/ since bar open >= D contracts |
| cdr | information | cumulative-delta-range bar: closes when (max - min) of the running cumulative delta inside the bar >= D |
| vnorm | information | variance-normalised bar: closes when sum of squared print-to-print price changes (ticks^2) >= trailing median daily RV / N; N bars per day-equivalent |
| cusum | information | CUSUM-filter events (Lopez de Prado): S+ = max(0,S+ + dp), S- = min(0,S- + dp) on print price changes; event when S+ > h or S- < -h, then reset; h = k x trailing sigma-scale (ticks) |
| dchg | information | directional-change / intrinsic-time events (Guillaume et al.): event when price reverses by theta ticks from the running extreme; bar = leg between events (stores overshoot beyond theta before the next reversal in column ovs via h |
| rng | price | true range bar: bar closes when a print would make high-low > N ticks; that print opens the next bar; known at that print |
| renko | price | classic Renko from prints: brick N ticks; reversal needs 2N from last close, new brick opens at last brick open |
| mrenko | price | median / mean Renko: brick N; reversal brick opens at the midpoint of last brick, triggers at 1.5N from last close |
| urenko | price | UniRenko: brick N, reversal brick opens at last close and closes N beyond (reversal 1N) |
| frenko | price | flex / hybrid Renko: trend brick N, reversal bar of 0.5N opening at last close |
| wrenko | price | Renko with wicks (BetterRenko style): classic Renko bricks whose high/low include the raw print extremes since the previous brick |
| kagi | price | Kagi line segments: reversal amount N ticks; bar per segment between reversals (shoulder/waist thickness change recorded by the segment extent) |
| pnf | price | point-and-figure column bars: box B ticks, reversal 3 boxes; one bar per column (open=start, close=end of column) |
| lbrk | price | three-line break and N-line break (N=3,4,5 in id lbrk_N_boxticks): line break charts on a base box of B ticks |
| swing | price | swing / zigzag bars: leg ends when price retraces pct (basis points of price) from the extreme (id swing_pct_bps) or ATR multiple (swing_atr_k, ATR of 5-min ranges trailing) |
| mom | price | momentum bars (NT8 MomentumBars): a bar closes when price has moved N ticks net in one direction from its open (bar spans the run; price pullback larger than the bar open resets the open) |
| trend | price | TrendBars (stated definition): gapless bars; trend bar closes at +N ticks beyond prior close in the trend direction; reversal bar opens at prior close and closes 2N against; wicks = raw print extremes; closes on the breaching prin |
| pasl | price | price-action swing legs: pivot confirmed by a retrace of max(N ticks, 0.5 x previous completed leg); bar = leg |
| anch | anchored | bars and VWAPs anchored at session open, 08:30, 09:30, prior-day high-volume node; equal-volume buckets from the anchor |
| sessrng | session-relative | session-relative equal-range: N bars per session each holding 1/N of the trailing typical session range |
| rngor | composite | range-or-time bar: closes on whichever comes first, range N ticks or T seconds (id rngor_N_T) |
| volor | composite | volume-or-range |
| dltor | composite | delta-or-range |
| ha | smoothing | Heikin-Ashi applied on top of any bar series above (ha_<base>) |
TRUST CHECKS (top 10 per session): independent brute-force tape walk vs harness trade-for-trade; 6 random truncation cuts must leave earlier signals unchanged; fills never earlier than signal time + 1 s; re-simulated L6M equals the grid cell. Dollars: points x $20 (the $4.84 round trip is already inside the points); live lots = max(1, floor(1.75 x base + 0.5)) = 2 for 1 base lot. Bracket points: 15u etc. are multiples of u (trailing 50-bar median bar range of that bar series, in points) and are CLIPPED to 10..120 pts: on wide bars (cdr, dib, ...) sym15u is simply 120/120.
A code like cdr_100 / dib_1200 / vol_350 / renko_14 = bar type + size (units in BAR_REGISTRY.md size grid; sizes scale with the session volume/range where the registry says so). Bracket codes: plain60/60 = stop 60 pts target 60 pts; sym15u / sym8u / sym30u = stop = target = 15/8/30 x u, u = trailing 50-bar median bar range (clipped 10..120 pts); asymA/B = stop A x u, target B x u.
